公司年度财务报告已经中兴华会计师事务所（特殊普通合伙）根据财政部颁布的《企业会计准则（2006）》审计，并出具了标准无保留意见的审计报告。

本年度报告及摘要中比较列报的以前年度财务数据已经中兴华会计师事务所（特殊普通合伙）根据财政部颁布的《企业会计准则（2006）》进行审计调整。

本年度报告中的财务数据为合并报表经审计后的数据，资本相关数据及主要经营指标按照监管要求合并报表口径计算得出。

公司董事长岳隆杰、行长朱鸣以及财务负责人段伟，保证年度报告中财务报告的真实、完整。

# IMPORTANT NOTES

The Board of Directors, the directors and senior management of the Company undertake that there are no misrepresentation, misleading statement, or material omission in the Report and its summary, and accept joint and several liability for the authenticity, accuracy and completeness of the contents of the Report.

The annual financial report of the company has been audited by Zhongxinghua Certified Public Accountants LLP according to the Accounting Standards for Enterprises (2006) promulgated by the Ministry of Finance and a standard unqualified auditor's opinion has been issued.

The financial data of previous years listed in this annual report and its summary for comparison have been audited and adjusted by Zhongxinghua Certified Public Accountants LLP according to the Accounting Standards for Enterprises (2006) promulgated by the Ministry of Finance.

The financial data listed in this annual report is the audited data of consolidated statements and capital related data has been calculated upon the standard of consolidated statements in accordance with the regulatory requirements.

Chairman Yue Longjie, President Zhu Ming and Finance Director Duan Wei confirm the authenticity and completeness of the financial statements in the Annual Report.

Board of Directors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd.

## 七、公司各类风险和风险管理情况

董事会、监事会、高级管理层、全面风险管理部门、风险管理主控部门、风险管理责任部门、内审部门及各分支机构组成公司全面风险管理体系。公司董事会承担风险管理的最终责任。高级管理层负责制定风险管理政策和程序，承担全面风险管理的实施责任。高级管理层下设信用风险审查委员会、资产负债管理委员会等专业委员会，并指定各类风险主控部门及管理部门。公司坚持“全员控风险”的风险管控理念，持续修订完善风险管理制度和操作规程，并实施了行之有效的风险管理和控制活动。报告期内，公司面临的主要风险有信用风险、市场风险、操作风险、流动性风险、信息科技风险等。

## (一) 信用风险管理

信用风险是指债务人或交易对手未能履行合同规定的义务或信用质量发生变化，影响金融产品价值，从而给债权人或金融产品持有人造成经济损失的风险。报告期内，公司从以下方面加强信用风险管理：一是强化授信政策导向作用。制定授信政策指引、信贷条线工作要点等，分类、分地施策，引导分支机构优化调整区域经营政策、市场营销政策、客户沙盘政策和信贷投向政策，提高信贷投放的精准性。同时，加强政策跟踪评价，确保授信政策与管控要求落实落地。二是健全信用风险管理体系。实施统一授信管理，将承担信用风险的表内外业务纳入统一授信管理；开展信用风险管理制度标准化建设，建立并实施信用风险预警处置机制；对拟授信项目实行延伸审查、交叉审查、涉及条线参与审查；加强评审队伍建设，提高内部培训针对性，积极引进评审条线专业人才；持续推进放款中心建设，强化授信、用信条件落实，严格审贷分离；建立贷后执行情况监测、检查常态机制；滚动式开展信用风险排查和专项检查，锁定风险苗头，前移风险管理关口。三是全力推进不良资产处置步伐。全面实施不良资产“334”战略，对不良贷款逐户制定方案，积极利用法律诉讼、资产转让、风险代理等方式妥善处置不良资产。

## （二）市场风险管理

市场风险是指因市场价格（利率、汇率、股票价格和商品价格）的不利变动，从而使银行表内外业务发生损失的风险。报告期内，公司从以下方面加强市场风险管理：一是完善市场风险管理体系。建立了与业务性质、规模、复杂程度相适应的市场风险管理体系，明确了“两会一层”、市场风险主控部门与风险承担部门的职责，制定了清晰的市场风险管理政策和程序，优化了识别、计量、监测及评估市场风险的工具和方法，明确了清晰的报告路线和信息披露方式。二是健全授权管理机制。根据不同业务的风险特征，明确交易与投资业务有权人的审批权限及交易员的操作权限，建立了前中后相对分

离的内部控制和监督机制，确保所有交易均能得到有效监督和操作。三是实行限额管理。建立包括资金交易限额、投资交易限额、交易账户总头寸限额、止损限额等在内的限额体系，对债券投资交易实行限额管理，并根据不同交易目的确定不同的市值重估频率，确保各类限额得到有效执行。四是优化资产配置结构。合理控制投资规模、类别及期限，适度压缩委外业务规模，掌握风险管理的主动权，有效规避市场价格波动带来的风险。五是严格实施外汇风险管控。实时关注客户结售汇情况，及时在外汇交易市场进行平盘交易，适当控制外汇敞口头寸和币种结构，有效管理汇率风险。

## (三)操作风险管理

操作风险是指由不完善或有问题的内部控制程序、员工、信息科技系统以及外部事件所造成损失的风险。报告期内，公司从以下方面加强操作风险管理：一是建立操作风险管理工具与平台。启动内控合规与操作风险管理系统建设，开展损失事件收集、关键风险指标确认和操作风险与控制自我评估工作，实施操作风险标准法资本测算，进一步提升操作风险精细化管理水平。二是抓实案件风险防控工作。制定案防工作要点，明确案件防控职责，主动查找风险隐患；开展员工行为排查，重点排查员工是否参与民间借贷、出借账户、非法集资等行为；印发员工家访管理办法，通过强化单位、家庭之间的联动，及时掌握员工思想波动；持续开展防范与处置非法集资宣传教育和专项排查工作，防范公司员工参与非法集资风险。三是强化重点领域风险排查。制定年度风险排查计划，对授权授信、同业业务、票据业务等重点领域和关键环节开展风险隐患排查工作，并对检查过程、效果实施监督评价，堵塞违规操作漏洞。四是开展全员思想教育活动。通过开展“守纪律讲规矩”主题党日、行长大讲堂、廉政警示教育、预防职务犯罪专题教育等多层次、多维度的思想教育活动，明确了员工行为“禁令”和操作“红线”，引导员工牢固树立红线意识和底线思维。

## (四)流动性风险管理

流动性风险是指银行虽然有清偿能力，但无法及时获得充足资金或无法以合理成本及时获得充足资金以应对资产增长或支付到期债务的风险。报告期内，公司从以下方面加强流动性风险管理：一是完善流动性风险管理体系。完善流动性风险管理政策流程，明确“两会一层”在流动性风险管理中的职责；建立流动性风险联动协作机制，形成流动性风险管控合力；建立完善流动性风险隔离和应急机制，防范流动性风险与其他风险的交叉传染。二是加强流动性风险指标监测报告。动态分析流动性比例、核心负债依存度、流动性缺口率等指标变动趋势，准确预测未来流动性需求，及时采取措施保障流动性充足；建立流动性风险日、周、月、年报监测报告机制，确保公司董事会、高级管理层及时了解流动性风险管理现状。三是定期开展压力测试。按季开展压力测试工作，综合考虑影响流动性风险传导的因素，采取动态流动性缺口模型实施压力测试，并根据测试结果适时修订流动性应急预案及调整资产负债管理策略。四是搭建流动性风险管理工具平台。年内建成上线资产负债管理系统，依托该系统逐步完善流动性风险管理与报告体系，提升系统在流动性风险管理中的应用水平。

## （五）信息科技风险及对策

信息科技风险是指信息科技在银行运用过程中，由于自然因素、人为因素、技术漏洞和管理缺陷产生的操作、法律和声誉等风险。报告期内，公司从以下方面加强信息科技风险管理：一是强化信息科技治理。明晰信息科技管理委员会的职能，厘清工作流程；修订信息科技制度44项，完善信息科技操作流程。二是完善信息科技风险管理工具。建立常态化的信息科技风险检测、预警与处置机制，充分应用信息科技风险管控系统，做好信息科技风险的持续跟踪监测。三是开展信息科技风险隐患排查。查摆风险管理流程及硬件设备方面隐患，并进行有效整改，持续完善信息系统基础建设。四是加强业务连续性应急能力建设。开展应急处置能力自查，建立重要领域风险排查清单；开展应急预案专项提升和统一备份专项梳理，整理应急预案58项，生产数据恢复验证49次；更新应急通讯录，与外包商、关联单位建立快速响应机制，提升业务演练的实用性。五是加强信息科技外包管理。严格落实信息科技外包安全管理措施，建立外包服务商评价和黑名单管理机制；开展外部服务商尽职调查，对外包活动的服务质量进行跟踪评价。六是强化信息安全管理。开展重要信息系统、互联网系统的安全评估，对电子银行、手机银行、门户网站等互联网应用系统进行渗透测试；开展各项网络安全自查和风险评估，实行内网准入控制，提升网络安全防控能力。七是加强客户信息保护。规范生产数据使用、提取和销毁等流程，提升客户信息安全性；部署外包虚拟化桌面环境，防止外包人员非授权获取数据信息；部署脱敏系统，有效防范敏感信息泄漏。

## 八、内部控制制度的完整性、合理性与有效性

公司持续以审慎经营、防范风险为出发点，本着全面、审慎、有效、独立的原则不断加强内部控制，全面贯彻执行各类内控制度，实现了各项业务的快速发展和稳健运行。

报告期内，公司进一步完善制度生成机制，成立了制度评审委员会，对各部门、各条线的内控制度进行审议；印发了制度建设管理办法，对内控制度生命周期进行全过程管理，制度的完整性、合理性、有效性显著提升。同时，公司将2017年确定为“制度建设年”，结合组织架构调整，在广泛征询意见的基础上修订完善368项制度，基本形成了内容科学、流程严密、运行有效的内部规章制度体系。

2017年，公司对内部控制制度的建设、执行落实情况进行了客观评价。根据评价结果，公司建立了能满足管理需要的内部控制体系，能结合发展需要不断改进和完善内部控制制度体系，相关制度覆盖了业务活动和内部管理的主要方面和关键环节，在规范财务会计行为、提高会计信息质量、强化经营管理、控制经营风险、堵塞漏洞、防止舞弊等方面得到了有效的执行。内部控制体系从内部控制环境、风险识别与评估、主要业务控制措施、信息交流与反馈、内部监督与纠正等方面，涵盖了内部控制的主要要素，内部控制体系基本完整合理，整体运行顺畅有效，基本达到内部控制的预期目标，内部控制体系不存在重大缺陷，符合监管要求和内部实际。

## VII. Risks of the Company and risk management

The comprehensive risk management system for the company's risk management consists of the Board of Directors, the Board of Supervisors, the senior management, the comprehensive risk management department, the main control department for risk management, the responsible department for risk management, the internal audit department, and all branches. The Board of Directors is ultimately responsible for risk management. The senior management is responsible for formulating risk management policies and procedures and implementing overall risk management. The senior management has set up special committees such as the Credit Risk Review Committee and the Assets-Liabilities Management Committee, and also designated the main control departments and management departments of various risks. Sticking to the idea of comprehensive risk management of "all staff involved in risk control", the Company has been constantly revising and perfecting the risk management system and operation procedures, and adopting the effective risk management and control measures. Major risks confronted by the Company during the reporting period include credit risk, market risk, operational risk, liquidity risk, IT risk, etc.

##### (i) Credit risk management

Credit risk refers to the risks of economic losses to the creditors or financial product holders caused by the influence on the value of financial products resulted from failure to fulfil the obligations stipulated in the contract of or changes of the credit quality of debtors or counterparties. During the reporting period, the Company strengthened the credit risk management from the following aspects. Firstly, the guiding role of the credit extension policies was enhanced. The guidelines of the credit extension policies and main work points of the credit business line were defined, and various branches were guided to optimize and adjust their regional business policies, marketing policies, customer sand policies, and credit investment policies through measures of different classification and for different places so that the precision of credit investment was improved. At the same time, the policy tracking and evaluation were strengthened to ensure the real implementation of the credit extension policies and control requirements. Secondly, the Company perfected the credit risk management system. All in-balance-sheet off-balance-sheet businesses which undertake credit risks were included in the unified credit management; the standardization construction was carried out for the credit risk management system, and the credit risk early-warning and disposal mechanism was established and implemented; extended and cross-review in which involved business lines participate was conducted for projects under credit extension; the construction of the review team was strengthened, the pertinence of the internal training was improved, and the professional talents of the review line were introduced actively; the construction of the disbursement center was promoted continuously, the implementation of credit extension and usage conditions was strengthened, and credit and loan was separated strictly; the normal mechanism of post-loan performance monitoring and check was established; testing and special inspection of credit risks were conducted in the rolling manner so as to lock risk signs and place risk management pass ahead. Thirdly, the Company promoted the disposal of non-performing assets vigorously. The "334" strategy was fully implemented for the non-performing assets so that a plan was made for each of the non-performing loans and those non-performing loans were disposed properly by actively using lawsuits, asset transfer, risk agency and otherwise.

##### (ii) Management of market risk

Market risk refers to the risks of losses of in-balance-sheet and off-balance-sheet businesses due to adverse changes in market price (interest rate, exchange rate, stock price and commodity price). During the reporting period, the Company strengthened the market risk management from the following aspects. Firstly, the Company perfected the market risk management system. The market risk management system was established to match with business nature, scale and complexity, make clear the responsibilities of "two boards and one level (the Board of Directors, the Board of Supervisors, and the management)", and main control and responsible departments of market risks, set up the clear market risk management policies and procedures, optimized the tools and methods to recognize, measure, monitor and evaluate.

market risks, and the explicit reporting route and way of information disclosure were defined. Secondly, the Company improved the authorization management mechanism. According to the risk characteristics of different businesses, the examination and approval authority of those have the right of trade and investment business and the operating authority of traders were defined clearly, and the internal control and supervision mechanism was established which is relatively separate in pre-, mid-, and post-authority to ensure effective supervision and operation of all transactions. Thirdly, the limit management was implemented. The limit system was set up, covering capital trading limit, investment trading limit, total position limit in trading accounts, stop-loss limit, and others; the limit management was implemented for bond investment trades, and different frequencies of revaluating market value were determined according to different trading purposes, so as to ensure all kinds of limits to get effective implementation. Fourthly, the Company optimized the asset allocation structure. The scale, category and duration of investment were controlled reasonably, and the outsourcing business scale was reduced moderately, so that the initiative of risk management was grasped, and the risks caused by market price fluctuations were avoided effectively. Fifthly, the Company strictly implemented the foreign exchange risk control by paying close attention to the situation of clients' settlement and sales of foreign exchange transactions in real time, carrying out flat trade in the foreign exchange market in a timely manner, and appropriately controlling the position of foreign exchange exposure and currency structure so as to effectively manage exchange rate risks.

##### (iii) Management of operational risk

Operational risk refers to the risks of loss caused by imperfect or problematic internal control procedures, employees, IT systems, or external events. During the reporting period, the Company strengthened the operational risk management from the following aspects. Firstly, the Company established the tools and platform of operational risk management by starting the construction of the internal control compliance and operational risk management system, carrying out loss event collection, confirmation of key risk indicators, and self-assessment of operational risks and their control, implementing the capital estimation with the standard operational risk method, and further enhancing the fine management level of operational risks. Secondly, the Company seized the prevention and control of case risks by defining the key points of case prevention and control, clarifying the responsibility of case prevention and control, and actively looking for hidden risks; by conducting the investigation of employees' behavior, with focus on whether employees participate in private lending, lending accounts, illegal fund-raising, and etc.; by printing and distributing the Management Measures for Home Visits to Employees, and timely understanding their thought fluctuations through strengthening the linkage between the unit and the family; by continuing to carrying out publicity, education and special inspection to prevent and deal with illegal fund-raising activities so as to prevent employees from participating in illegal fund-raising risks. Thirdly, the Company strengthened risk screening in key areas by developing the annual risk screening plan, screening hidden risks in authorization and credit extension, peer business, bill business, and other key areas and crucial links, supervising and evaluating those inspection process and effect to plug up loopholes in irregularities. Fourthly, the Company carried out ideological education activities of all employees by conducting the thematic party day of "observing disciplines and rules", Lecture Hall of President, integrity and warning education, special education of preventing duty-related crimes, and other multi-level and multi-dimensional ideological education activities, so as to clearly define the "behavior ban" and "operation red line" of employees and guide staff to firmly establish the red line consciousness and the bottom line thought.

#### (iv) Management of liquidity risk

Liquidity risk refers to the risks of inability to timely obtain sufficient funds or timely obtain sufficient funds at a reasonable cost to deal with asset growth or pay due debts although the bank has solvency. During the reporting period, the Company strengthened the liquidity risk management from the following aspects. Firstly, the Company improved the liquidity risk management system by perfecting the liquidity risk management policy process and clarifying the responsibilities of the "two boards and one level" in the liquidity risk management; by establishing the linkage and coordination mechanism for liquidity risks to form the combined force for liquidity risk control; by setting up the complete liquidity risk isolation and emergency mechanism to prevent cross-infection of liquidity risks and other

risks. Secondly, the Company strengthened the monitoring and reporting of liquidity risk indicators by dynamically analyzing the change trends of liquidity ratio, core debt dependence, liquidity gap rate and other indicators, accurately forecasting future liquidity demand, and timely taking measures to ensure sufficient liquidity; by establishing the daily, weekly, monthly and yearly monitoring and reporting mechanism for liquidity risks to ensure that the Company's Board of Directors and senior management had a timely understanding of the current situation of liquidity risk management. Thirdly, the Company conducted regular stress tests by doing at a quarterly basis, comprehensively considering the factors influencing the conduction of liquidity risks, adopting the dynamic liquidity gap model to implement stress tests, and timely revising the liquidity contingency plan and adjusting the assets and liabilities management strategy according to the test results. Fourthly, the Company built a liquidity risk management tool platform. In 2017, the online asset & liability management system was established, based on which the Company gradually improved the management and reporting system of liquidity risks and improved the application level of the system in liquidity risk management.

##### (v) IT risks and their countermeasures

Information technology (IT) risk refers to the operational, legal and reputation risks of information technology due to natural factors, human factors, technical loopholes and management defects in the application process in banks. During the reporting period, the Company strengthened the IT risk management from the following aspects. Firstly, the Company enhanced the IT governance by clarifying the functions and working process of the IT Management Committee; by revising 44 IT systems and improving the IT operation process. Secondly, the Company improved the IT risk management tools by establishing the normalized mechanism for detection, early warning and disposal of IT risks, and making full use of the IT risk control system to ensure the continuous tracking and monitoring of IT risks. Thirdly, the Company screened hidden IT risks by checking hidden risks in risk management process and hardware equipment, carrying out effective rectification, and continuously improving the information system infrastructure. Fourthly, the Company strengthened the building of the business continuity emergency capacity by carrying out self-inspection of emergency response capacity and preparing a list of risks screened in key areas; by specially improving the emergency plans and unified backup plans through sorting 58 emergency plans and verifying 49 times of production data recovery; by updating the emergency address book, establishing the quick response mechanism with the outsourcers and affiliated units, and improving the practicability of the business practice. Fifthly, the Company strengthened the management of IT outsourcing by strictly implementing the security management measures for IT outsourcing, and establishing the evaluation on and blacklist management mechanism of outsourcing service providers; by carrying out due diligence of external service providers, and tracking and evaluating the service quality of outsourcing activities. Sixthly, the Company enhanced the information security management by carrying out security assessment on important information systems and Internet systems, and conducting penetration tests on Internet applications such as electronic banking, mobile banking and portal websites; by making self-inspection and risk assessment of network security, implementing the access control for the internal network, and improving the capability of cyber security prevention and control. Seventhly, the Company strengthened customer information protection by standardizing the usage, extraction and destruction procedures of production data to improve customer information security; by deploying the virtualization desktop environment of outsourcing to prevent outsourcing personnel from unauthorized access to data information, and the desensitization system to effectively prevent leakage of sensitive information.

## VIII. Completeness, reasonableness and effectiveness of the internal control system

Starting from prudent operation and risk prevention, the Company continuously strengthened internal control, comprehensively implemented various internal control systems and achieved rapid development and stable operation of all businesses in accordance with comprehensive, prudent, effective and independent principle.

During the reporting period, the Company further improved the system generation mechanism

by establishing the System Review Committee, and deliberating the internal control systems of all departments and business lines; printing and releasing the Management Measures for System Construction, and carrying out the whole-process management of the internal control system life cycle, so as to significantly improve the completeness, reasonableness and effectiveness of the system. At the same time, the Company defined 2017 as the "Year of System Construction", revised and improved 368 systems in combination with the adjustment of the organizational structure on the basis of widespread consultation, so that an institutional system of internal control rules and regulations was formed basically which is scientific in content, rigorous in process, and effective in operation.

In 2017, the Company made an objective evaluation on the construction and implementation of the internal control system and set up the internal control system meeting the needs of management according to the evaluation results, so that the internal control system was continuously improved and perfected in combination with the needs of development, the related systems covered the major aspects and key links of the business activities and internal management, and also was effectively implemented in standardizing financial accounting behavior, improving the quality of accounting information, strengthening the operating management, controlling operational risks, plugging loopholes, preventing fraud, and so on. In addition, the internal control system has covered the key elements of internal control from the aspects of internal control environment, risk identification and evaluation, control measures for main business, information communication and feedback, internal supervision and correction, etc.; the internal control system is basically reasonable free of major defects, complies with the regulatory requirements and internal reality, operates smoothly and efficiently overall, and basically achieves the anticipated target of internal control.

## （五）公司薪酬架构情况

### 1. 薪酬管理架构及决策程序

公司建立了科学的薪酬管理架构，明确规定董事、监事的报酬由股东大会决定，董事会决定高级管理人员的报酬。董事会对本行薪酬管理负最终责任，负责审阅、批准、修订本行薪酬管理制度与政策、薪酬成本与预算等，监督本行薪酬制度实施情况，并定期评估本行薪酬机制的健全性和有效性。董事会下设提名与薪酬考核委员会，负责审议有关薪酬制度、政策及监督工作。高级管理层负责实施董事会薪酬管理方面的决议，组织制定并更新本行薪酬管理制度与政策，管理和控制薪酬预算与成本，审阅、批准员工的定薪、调薪方案、绩效考核方案、员工福利方案等。

### 2. 薪酬与业绩衡量、风险调整的标准

公司总行部室员工绩效工资按照《莱商银行总行部室绩效考核管理办法》规定进行考核后兑现，分支行绩效工资按照《莱商银行绩效考核管理办法》进行考核后兑现。

### 3. 薪酬制度情况

以市场最佳实践为指引，确立了“市场导向、岗位导向、绩效导向、能力导向”的付薪理念，于2016年开展了薪酬体系优化设计项目，根据员工岗位职责及标准进行人岗匹配，实行了以岗定薪、按月拨付的固定薪酬制度，将薪酬总额与全行经营利润挂钩，建立自上而下的薪酬总额挂钩经营利润的提取机制，以及自下而上的薪酬总额核算机制，逐步健全全行薪酬总额管控机制。

明确了管理人员绩效薪酬延期支付相关规定，根据管理人员不同层级及岗位差异，将其绩效薪酬与工作责任和风险防控挂钩，并按照一定比例延期支付。

### 4. 年度薪酬方案制定及考核完成情况

公司年度薪酬方案根据人员规模、业务发展情况等制定，2017年度，公司各项经营指标良好，薪酬情况合理的反映了公司业绩及风险管理情况。

5. 超出原定薪酬方案的例外情况

报告期内，公司无超出原定薪酬的例外情况。

## 二、员工情况

报告期末，公司在职员工人数为1795人；员工中本科及以上学历1733人，占比96.55%。

##### (v) Remunerations structure of the Company

### 1. Remunerations management structure and decision-making procedure

The company has established a scientific remuneration management structure, clearly stipulating that the remuneration of directors and supervisors shall be decided by the general meeting of shareholders, and the Board of Directors shall decide the remuneration of senior managers; the Board of Directors is ultimately responsible for the salary management of the Bank, responsible for reviewing, approving, revising the compensation management system and policies, and remuneration cost and budget of the Bank, supervising the implementation of the Bank's remuneration system, and regularly assessing the soundness and effectiveness of the remuneration mechanism; under the Board of Directors is the Nomination and Remuneration Assessment Committee, which is responsible for reviewing work relating to the remuneration system, policy and supervision; senior management is responsible for the implementation of resolutions of the Board of Directors about the remuneration management, organizing to develop and update the remuneration management system and policies, managing and controlling salary budget and cost, reviewing and approving the employees' wage fixing and salary increase plan, performance appraisal plan and staff benefit plan, etc.

2. Remunerations and the standard of performance measurement and risk adjustment

Performance salary of the department / office employees of the Company’s head office shall be paid based on appraisal according to the provisions of the "Management Measures for Performance Appraisal of Department / Office of Laishang Bank's Head Office", and that of branch employees based on the provisions of the "Management Measures for Performance Appraisal of Laishang Bank".

3. Remuneration system

The Company has implemented new salary system (grading system) since 2010. On the principle of “responsibility-based position, position setting simplification and specialization”, the Company implemented the person-post matching based on job responsibilities of employees and standards, fixed salary confirmed by post and paid monthly and delay in payment of performance salary.

4. Preparation of annual remuneration plan and completion of assessment

The Company’s annual remuneration plan was prepared according to the size of staff, business development, and etc. In 2016, various operational indicators of the Company were good, and the remunerations reasonably reflected the performance and risk management of the Company.

5. Exceptions beyond the original remuneration plan

There was no exception beyond the original remuneration plan in the Company in the reporting period.

#### II. Employee profile

At the end of the reporting period, there were 1,795 employees in the Company, among whom employees with a bachelor's degree or above amounted to 1,733, accounting for 96.55%.

## 第五章 公司治理结构

## 一、公司治理情况

报告期内，公司严格遵守《公司法》和《商业银行法》的有关规定，认真落实监管部门关于公司治理的有关规定，致力于进一步完善公司治理结构，增强公司治理机制的有效性，提高董事会决策的科学性。公司治理具体情况如下：

（一）关于股东与股东大会。公司按照《公司章程》和《股东大会议事规则》的要求召集、召开股东大会，积极听取股东的意见和建议，确保股东对公司重大事项的知情权、参与权和表决权。报告期内，公司召开了2016年度股东大会、2017年第一次临时股东大会、2017年第二次临时股东大会，会议严格按照有关规定履行了相关法律程序，保证了股东参会并行使权利，并由律师出具了法律意见书。

（二）关于董事和董事会。公司现有董事11名，其中独立董事3名。董事会能够严格按照《公司章程》的规定，认真履行职责，在股东大会授权下进行决策，强化公司治理，推进战略步伐，促进稳健经营，维护了公司和全体股东的利益。报告期内公司共召开了8次董事会会议。

目前，公司董事会下设1个办公室和5个专门委员会，包括董事会办公室、关联交易控制委员会、风险管理委员会、战略发展委员会、审计委员会、提名与薪酬考核委员会，其中风险管理委员会下设声誉风险管理专业委员会和信息科技风险管理专业委员会。5个专门委员会具有明确的职权范围，监管公司不同方面的事务，并对董事会负责，为董事会决策提供了建设性建议，进一步提高了董事会决策的科学性。

（三）关于监事和监事会。公司现有监事8人，其中职工监事3人，外部监事2人。监事会按照《公司章程》的规定认真履行职责，对公司财务、风险管理、资产质量以及部分高级管理人员履职的合规性进行监督检查，定期及不定期地与外部审计机构和管理层进行情况交流，有针对性地开展专项检查，针对风险隐患和发现的问题，向董事会和管理层提出建议和改进意见，从而更加有效地发挥监事会的作用。

（四）关于信息披露与透明度。公司在《金融时报》上发布了2016年度报告，并制作了《莱商银行股份有限公司2016年度报告》（摘要）单行本，及时准确地进行信息披露，切实维护广大股东特别是中小投资者的利益。

## 二、独立董事及履行职责情况

报告期内，公司董事会现有独立董事3人，独立董事本着为全体股东负责的态度，履行诚信和勤勉义务，认真参加董事会及各专门委员会会议，积极发表意见，维护公司整体利益及中小股东

的合法权益不受侵犯。

（一）独立董事参加会议情况。报告期内，独立董事应参加董事会会议21人次，亲自出席20人次，委托出席1人次，缺席0人次。

（二）独立董事对公司有关事项提出异议情况。报告期内，公司独立董事未对公司本年度董事会的议案提出异议。

## 三、外部监事及履行职责情况

报告期内，公司监事会现有外部监事2人，外部监事本着为全体股东负责的态度，履行诚信和勤勉义务，认真参加监事会及各专门委员会会议，积极发表意见，维护公司整体利益及中小股东的合法权益不受侵犯。

（一）外部监事参加会议情况。报告期内，公司监事应参加监事会会议8人次，亲自出席8人次，委托出席0人次，缺席0人次。

（二）外部监事对公司有关事项提出异议情况。报告期内，公司外部监事未对公司本年度董事会、监事会的议案提出异议。

## 四、公司经营决策体系

公司最高权力机构是股东大会，通过董事会、监事会进行管理和监督。行长受聘于董事会，对公司日常经营管理全面负责。公司实行一级法人体制，各分支机构均为非独立核算单位，其经营管理活动根据公司授权进行，并对公司负责。

公司无控股股东。公司与大股东在业务、人员、资产、机构、财务五方面完全独立。公司具有独立完整的业务及自主经营能力，董事会、监事会和内部机构能够独立运作。

## 五、高级管理人员考评及激励机制、制度情况

报告期内，公司董事会进一步完善了对高级管理人员的绩效考核管理办法，建立了薪酬与公司效益和个人业绩相联系的激励约束机制；考核实行百分制，根据各高管人员的工作性质不同，考核项目有所侧重。对于非执行董事、非职工监事的考核主要以其出席会议次数及履职能力考核为主。制定了《莱商银行股份有限公司董事会董事履职评价制度》，主要分为客观评价、自我评价和相互评价三个部分，根据各项指标具体内容的不同，给予不同的比重。公司制定了《莱商银行股份有限公司董事会对高管人员履职评价办法》、《莱商银行股份有限公司监事会对董事、监事、高管人员履职评价办法》、《关于追究董事、监事及高管人员责任的规定》。同时，公司监事会进一步量化考核标准，制定了《莱商银行股份有限公司监事会对高管人员履职评价办法实施细则》，明确了监事会对高级管理人员的履职评价，加大了履职问责力度。

### Chapter V Corporate Governance Structure

## I. Corporate governance of the Company

During the reporting period, the Company has strictly observed the related provisions in the Company Law and the Law on Commercial Banks, conscientiously implemented the related regulations of the regulatory intuition on corporate governance, and been dedicated to further improving corporate governance structure, enhancing the effectiveness of the corporate governance mechanism and making the decision of the Board of Directors more scientific. The performance of the Company in its corporate governance is as follows:

##### (i) Shareholders and shareholders' general meeting.

The Company has convened the shareholders’ general meeting in accordance with the requirement of the Articles of Association and the Rules of Procedures for Shareholders’ Meeting and actively listened to shareholders’ opinion and suggestion to ensure that all shareholders have the right to know, to participate and to vote to the major events of the Company. During the reporting period, the Company held its 2016 annual shareholders’ general meeting, 2017 first interim shareholders’ general meeting, and 2017 second interim shareholders’ general meeting. The meetings went through related legal procedures in strict accordance with the related regulations to ensure that shareholders participate in the meetings and exercise their right. Also, legal opinion was issued by a lawyer.

##### (ii) Directors and the Board of Directors.

Currently, the Company has 11 directors, including 3 independent directors. The Board of Directors is able to conscientiously perform its duties in strict accordance with the provisions in the Articles of Association, make decisions with the authorization of the shareholders' general meeting, strengthen the corporate governance, advance strategic steps and promote steady operation which safeguarded the interests of the Company and all shareholders. During the reporting period, the Company held 8 board meetings totally.

Currently, under the Board of Directors of the Company is there 1 office and 5 special committees, including the Office of Board of Directors, Related Transaction Control Committee, Risk Management Committee, Strategic Development Committee, Audit Committee, and Nomination and Remuneration Assessment Committee. Under Risk Management Committee are the Special Committee of Reputation Risk Management and Special Committee of IT Risk Management. These 5 special committees have specific scopes of power, supervise and manage the matters at different aspects, and are accountable to the Board of Directors. They provide constructive suggestions for the decision making of the Board of Directors in a timely manner to improve the science of the decisions made by the Board of Directors.

##### (iii) Supervisors and the Board of Supervisors.

The Company has 8 supervisors, including 3 employee supervisors and 2 external supervisors. The Board of Supervisors conscientiously performs its duties in strict accordance with the provisions in the Articles of Association, carries out supervision and inspection over the compliance of the Company's finance, risk management, asset quality and the duty performance of the senior management, communicates with the external audit units and management regularly or irregularly, conducts targeted special inspections, and puts forward suggestions and improvement opinions to the Board of Directors and the management according to hidden risks or problems discovered, so as to play the role of the Board of Supervisors more effectively.

##### (iv) Information disclosure and transparency.

The Company has published its 2016 Annual Report on Financial Times, and prepared a separate edition of 2016 Annual Report of Laishang Co., Ltd. (Summary), to carry out information disclosure in a timely and accurate manner and safeguard the interests of shareholders, particularly medium and small shareholders.

## II. Independent directors and their performance of duties

During the reporting period, there were 3 independent directors on the Board of Directors. With an attitude of being responsible to all shareholders, these independent directors performed the obligation of integrity and diligence, earnestly participated in the board meetings and meetings of various special committees, and actively delivered their opinions to safeguard the collective interest of the Company and the legitimate rights and interest of medium and small shareholders.

##### (i) Independent directors' participation in meetings.

During the reporting period, the number of independent directors attending the board meetings should be 21, with presence in 20 persons, entrusted attendance in 1 persons, and absence in 0 person.

##### (ii) Objections of the independent directors to related affairs of the Company.

During the reporting period, the independent directors of the Company did not object to any proposal of the Board of Directors of the current year.

## III. External supervisors and their performance of duties

There were 2 external supervisors in the Board of Supervisors in the Company during the report period. With an attitude of being responsible to all shareholders, these external supervisors performed their duty of integrity and diligence, carefully participated in the meeting of the Board of Supervisors and all special committee meetings, positively gave opinions and prevent overall benefit of the Company and lawful rights and interests of medium and small shareholders from infringement.

##### (i) Presence of External Supervisors.

During the reporting period, the number of the external supervisors attending the Board of

Supervisors Meeting should be 8, with presence in 8 persons, entrusted attendance in 0 person and absence in 0 person.

## (ii) Objection raised by the External Supervisor to related affairs of the Company.

In the reporting period, the external supervisors of the Company did not raise objections to the proposals of the Board of Directors and Board of Supervisors of the Company of the current year.

## IV. Operation and decision-making system of the Company

The highest authority of the Company is the Shareholders' General Meeting, which is managed and supervised through the Board of Directors and the Board of Supervisors. The president, employed by the Board of Directors, is responsible for all daily operation and management of the Company. Only the head office of the Company is its legal person, and all branches and sub-branches are non-independent accounting units, whose operation and management activities shall be carried out with the authorization of the Company and who shall be responsible for the Company.

The Company has no controlling shareholder. The Company and its majority shareholders are completely independent in terms of business, personnel, assets, organization and finance. The Company has independent and complete business and independent operation ability, and the Board of Directors, the Board of Supervisors and internal organizations can operate independently.

## V.Assessment and incentive mechanism and system of senior management

During the reporting period, the Board of Directors of the Company further improved the method of performance assessment and management of its senior management, and established an incentive and constraint mechanism in which remunerations are associated with the performance of the Company and personal performance; the centesimal system has been implemented for assessment subject to the working nature of senior management, and different management positions have different key points for assessment. The assessment of non-executive directors and non-employee supervisors has mainly focused on their attendance on meetings and ability to perform their duties. The Evaluation System of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. on Duty Performance of Directors of the Board of Directors, which includes three parts, i.e., objective evaluation, self-evaluation and mutual evaluation, has been prepared. Different proportions are given according to different contents of various indexes. The Measures of the Board of Directors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. on Assessment of Senior Management's Performance, the Measures of the Board of Supervisors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. on Assessment of Directors, Supervisors and Senior Management's Performance and the Provisions of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. on Accountability of Directors, Supervisors and Senior Management have been formulated. Meanwhile, the Board of Supervisors further quantified the criteria of assessment, formulated the Implementing Rules of Duty Performance Assessment Method on Senior Management by the Board of Supervisors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., specified the duty performance assessment on senior management by the Board of Supervisors and strengthened the accountability of duty performance.

## 第六章 股东大会情况

报告期内公司共召开了三次股东大会，具体情况如下：

2017年4月28日，在公司综合营业楼一楼礼堂召开了2016年度股东大会，出席会议的股东及股东代理人共50名，代表股份169142.9556万股，占公司总股本的84.57%。大会审议通过了《莱商银行股份有限公司董事会2016年度工作报告》《莱商银行股份有限公司监事会2016年度工作报告》《莱商银行股份有限公司2016年度审计报告》《莱商银行股份有限公司2016年度财务报告及利润分配方案》《关于<莱商银行股份有限公司2017年度预算>的议案》《莱商银行股份有限公司2016年度内部人和股东关联交易执行情况的报告》《莱商银行股份有限公司2016年度风险管理报告》《关于对〈莱商银行股份有限公司2016年度报告〉进行信息披露的议案》《莱商银行股份有限公司监事会对董事2016年度履职评价报告》《莱商银行股份有限公司监事会对高级管理人员2016年度履职评价报告》《莱商银行股份有限公司监事会对监事2016年度履职评价报告》《关于上海浦东发展银行将持有的莱商银行股份有限公司18%股权转让给齐鲁交通发展集团有限公司的议案》《关于增持天津武清村镇银行股份有限公司股份的议案》《关于修改<莱商银行股份有限公司股东大会议事规则>的议案》《莱商银行股份有限公司2017年机构发展规划》《关于2016年度拟核销不良贷款的议案》《关于调整菏泽分行新办公楼购置总价款以及置换土地税费的议案》《关于对莱芜钢铁集团有限公司及其关联企业综合授信的议案》《关于对新汶矿业集团有限责任公司及其关联企业综合授信的议案》《莱商银行股份有限公司2017年增资扩股方案》《关于高管人员薪酬调整情况的报告》《关于选举朱鸣为董事的议案》《关于选举胡天晓为监事的议案》。

2017年8月15日，在公司综合营业楼六楼会议室召开了2017年第一次临时股东大会，出席会议的股东及股东代理人共47名，代表股份173695.5388万股，占公司总股本的86.85%。大会审议通过了《关于陈海宁等董事辞职的议案》《关于选举王启祥为第四届董事会董事的议案》《关于增持五台莱商村镇银行股份有限公司股份的议案》《关于增持河南方城凤裕村镇银行有限责任公司股份的议案》《莱商银行股份有限公司2017年二季度内部人和股东关联交易执行情况的报告》《莱商银行股份有限公司2017年二季度风险管理报告》。

2017年12月21日，在在公司综合营业楼六楼会议室召开了2017年第二次临时股东大会，出席会议的股东及股东代理人共50名，代表股份168075.4454万股，占公司总股本的84.04%。大会审议通过了《莱商银行股份有限公司关于实施股权托管的议案》《关于齐鲁交通发展集团有限公司增持股份的议案》《关于2017年增资扩股情况的报告》《关于变更莱商银行股份有限公司注册资本的议案》《关于修改<莱商银行股份有限公司章程>的议案》《关于李尊富董事申请辞职的议案》《关于选举吕慎宾为第四届董事会董事的议案》《关于<莱商银行股份有限公司2018年机构发展规划>的议案》《关于2017年度拟核销不良贷款的议案》《关于<莱商银行股份有限公司三年资本规

划（2018-2020）>的议案》《莱商银行股份有限公司2017年3季度内部人和股东关联交易执行情况报告》《关于<莱商银行股份有限公司关联交易管理办法>的议案》《莱商银行股份有限公司2017年3季度风险管理报告》《关于集中化解部分不良资产的议案》。

### Chapter VI Shareholders' General Meeting

Shareholders’ general meetings of the Company have been held for 3 times during the reporting period.

On April 28, 2017, the 2016 Shareholders' General Meeting was held in the auditorium on the first floor of the comprehensive business building of the Company. 50 shareholders and authorized agents attended the meeting, representing 1,691,429,556 shares, accounting for 84.57% of the Company's total share capital. The meeting deliberated and passed the 2016 Annual Work Report of the Board of Directors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., 2016 Annual Work Report of the Board of Supervisors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., 2016 Annual Audit Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., 2016 Annual Financial Report and Profit Distribution Plan of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Proposal on 2017 Annual Budget of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., 2016 Annual Report on Executive Condition of Related Transactions by Insiders and Shareholders of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., 2016 Annual Risk Management Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Proposal on Information Disclosure for '2016 Annual Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd.' Evaluation Report on 2016 Annual Duty Performance of Directors by the Board of Supervisors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Evaluation Report on 2016 Annual Duty Performance of Senior Management by the Board of Supervisors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Evaluation Report on 2016 Annual Duty Performance of Supervisors by the Board of Supervisors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Proposal on Transfer of 18% Shareholding Held in Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. from Shanghai Pudong Development Bank Co., Ltd. to Qilu Transportation Development Group Co., Ltd., Proposal on Increase in Shareholding in Tianjin Wuqing Village Bank Co., Ltd., Proposal on Modification of Rules of Procedures for Shareholders' Meeting of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., 2017 Annual Development Planning of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Proposal on Write-off of Non-performing Loans of 2016, Proposal on Adjustment of Total Price of Purchasing New Office Building for Heze Branch and Replacement Land Tax, Proposal on Comprehensive Credit Extension for Laiwu Steel Group Co., Ltd. and Its Affiliated Enterprises, Proposal on Comprehensive Credit Extension for Xinwen Mining Group Co., Ltd. and Its Affiliated Enterprises, 2017 Program of Capital Increase and Share Expansion of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Report on Adjustment of Senior Management's Remuneration, Proposal on Electing Zhu Ming as Director, and Proposal on Electing Hu Tianxiao as Supervisor.

On August 15, 2017, the First Interim Shareholders' General Meeting in 2017 was held in the meeting room on the sixth floor of the comprehensive business building of the Company. 47 shareholders and authorized agents attended the meeting, representing 1,736,955,388 shares, accounting for 86.85% of the Company's total share capital. The meeting deliberated and passed the Proposal on Resignation of Chen Haining and Other Directors, Proposal on Electing Wang Qixiang as Director of the Fourth Board of Directors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Proposal on Increase in Shareholding in Wutai Laishang Village Bank Co., Ltd., Proposal on Increase in Shareholding in Henan Fangcheng Fengyu Village Bank LLC., Report on Executive Condition of Related Transactions by Insiders and Shareholders of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. in Q2 2017, and Risk

Management Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd of Q2 2017.

On December 21, 2017, the Second Interim Shareholders' General Meeting in 2017 was held in the meeting room on the sixth floor of the comprehensive business building of the Company. 50 shareholders and authorized agents attended the meeting, representing 1,680,754,454 shares, accounting for 84.04% of the Company's total share capital. The meeting deliberated and passed the Proposal on Implementing Equity Trusteeship of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Program of Capital Increase and Share Expansion of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Proposal on Approving Qilu Transportation Development Group Co., Ltd. to Increase its Shares in Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., 2017 Report on Capital Increase and Share Expansion of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Proposal on Modification of Registered Capital of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Proposal on Modification of Articles of Association of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Proposal on Application of Resignation of Director Li Zunfu, Proposal on Electing Lv Shenbin as Director of the Fourth Board of Directors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Proposal on '2018 Annual Organizational Development Planning of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Proposal on Write-off of Non-performing Loans of 2017, Proposal on Three-Year (2018-2020) Development Planning of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Report on Executive Condition of Related Transactions by Insiders and Shareholders of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. in Q3 2017, Proposal on Administrative Measures for Related Transactions of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Risk Management Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. in Q3 2017, and Proposal on Concentrated Resolving Some Bad Assets.

## 第七章 董事会报告

## 一、2017年度主要经营管理情况

## （一）资产规模突破千亿元，经营成果明显提高

截至2017年12月31日，公司资产总额1030.34亿元，各项存款余额668.57亿元，各项贷款余额468.72亿元，公司实现拨备前利润（税前）12.70亿元，提取拨备7.10亿元，利润总额5.60亿元，缴纳税金5.31亿元，每股净资产2.91元，发展增速位居山东城商行前列，盈利水平显著提升，各类风险得到有效控制，各分支机构百花齐放，莱商事业呈现出朝气蓬勃、欣欣向荣的新气象。

## （二）发挥战略决策职能，持续提升发展能力

一是准确把握战略机遇，确保稳中有进的经营发展态势。董事会站位大局，把握国家关于金融改革创新和服务实体经济的新要求，把握重要战略机遇期内涵的深刻变化，把握国内外金融格局与金融监管的发展趋势，把握新常态下经营发展新的阶段性特征，科学确定可行的年度经营计划和经营管理方针，以经营转型为核心，以信贷经营为重心，以客户服务为基础，坚持稳健经营，不断开创改革发展新局面，促进经营发展能力的跃升。二是坚持创新服务，提高支持实体经济的质效。董事会坚守支持实体经济的本源，全力构建协调、可持续的转型发展模式，努力加快金融产品和服务模式创新，持续提升对实体经济的金融服务水平，适应经济转型升级与供给侧结构性改革需求，加大对重点建设项目的支持力度，加大对新旧动能转换领域的信贷支持，支持重

点企业做强做大，培育经济发展新动能。三是完善组织架构和网点布局。平稳顺利完成内部架构改革，总行部室由19个优化归并为15个，部室负责人全部重新竞聘上岗，非管理岗位人员全部参加双向选择、人岗匹配，进一步提高了运营效率。同时稳步推进分支机构建设。2017年，临沂蒙阴支行、日照开发区支行、济南龙奥支行、菏泽国花支行、曹县庄寨小微支行等多家支行完成开业。异地分支机构存款、贷款、拨备前利润均占到全行的75%左右，对全行发展的支撑作用越来越强。四是加强对外投资的管理。一方面我行对四家村镇银行和投资的其他机构加强了董事会、股东大会的议案审核，并出具审核意见，由派驻董事和授权代理人在会议上发表意见，规范了管理流程。另一方面我行增加对村镇银行的支持，选派优秀管理人员驻行参与经营管理和重大决策，从重要岗位和决策环节上强化了管理，目前共向村镇银行派驻管理人员17名，均在经营管理中发挥了重要作用。

## （三）加强全面风险管理，资产质量总体保持稳定

一是推进内控合规体系建设。扎实开展制度建设年活动，突出制度的科学性、适用性和实效性，共修订完成368项制度，基本形成了内容科学、流程严密、运行有效的内部规章制度体系。有计划地开展内部审计，年内完成了信贷资产质量真实性、条线业务、业务授权、薪酬管理、流动性管理、财务预算执行、消费者权益保护等方面的专项审计，有效掌握了行内经营管理中存在的问题，并提出整改措施，通过加强监督落实，确保依法合规经营。完成了2016年度全行内部控制评价工作。根据《商业银行内部控制指引》等监管要求，在内部控制日常监督和专项监督的基础上，完成了全行内部控制评价报告，评价内容主要涵盖了莱商银行概况、内部控制管理与发展、内部控制评价工作情况及内部控制有效性结论四个方面，较为客观地评价了我行的内部控制总体状况，为管理层提出了有效意见及建议。二是扎实推进资产质量攻坚工作，加大不良资产处置力度。在市委、市政府和银监分局的大力支持下，我行扎实推进资产质量攻坚行动，逐笔逐户落实处置责任人和处置方案，与公安、法院、不动产等相关部门通力合作，切实推进信用风险化解工作。三是强化关联交易管理。董事会进一步加强对关联交易的管理，对莱芜钢铁集团有限公司、新汶矿业集团有限公司及其关联企业进行了综合授信，高级管理层按照季度向董事会报告关联交易报告，并在内部办公自动化系统对员工和关系人授信情况进行公示，重新制定了《关联交易管理办法》，对关联法人和关联自然人进行了重新界定，推动了关联交易精细化、规范化管理机制的建设，进一步明确管理路径，保证了关联交易的合规性。四是建设和完善全面风险管理体系。完善风险管理政策、制度体系。围绕全行发展战略规划，制定了《莱商银行2017年全面风险管理政策》，明确了七大条线风险管理政策、策略及风险控制目标。强化信用风险管理系统应用。充分发挥信用风险管理系统的风险识别及预警功能，通过对风险预警信息的分析研判和及时处置，提升全行信用风险管控能力。开展内控合规与操作风险管理系统建设。通过系统建设，进一步整合内控合规与操作风险管理资源，完善内控合规与操作风险管理的方法、平台和工具，及时识别、评估、监测、报告、控制操作风险。

## （四）强化资本管理，资本充足率总体稳定

一是顺利实施10亿股增资扩股。在各级监管部门指导下，在股东的大力支持下，2017年12月莱商银行顺利完成10亿股增资扩股工作，募集资本金27.5亿元，为支持实体经济转调升级、促进全行高质量稳健发展提供了坚实的资本支撑。二是圆满完成战略投资者更换工作。在市委、市政府和山东银监局、莱芜银监分局的支持推动下，2017年1月，齐鲁交通发展集团受让上海浦发银行所持有的全部莱商银行股份，成为我行新的战略投资者。目前双方在业务融合、金融创新等领域的合作已经全面展开，下一步将重点推动战略合作的具体化、项目化，充分发挥战略投资者作用，努力打造济莱协作的典范。三是继续强化资本规划。为进一步提高资本使用效率，完善资本补充和约束机制，促进业务健康、持续、快速发展，董事会以最低资本监管要求为出发点，结合公司实际情况，预留资本缓冲空间，设定最优资本目标，重新对相关指标进行了测算，预计未来三年的资本缺口，并设定资本充足指标的预警值，提出资本补充计划，形成了《莱商银行股份有限公司三年资本规划(2018-2020)》。

## （五）积极履行社会责任，提升品牌价值

一是持续加大精准扶贫、普惠金融工作力度。紧跟各级党委、政府精准扶贫战略部署，充分发挥金融扶贫的杠杆作用，持续完善顶层设计，积极创新金融精准扶贫模式，通过精准对接金融需求、精准支持扶贫龙头企业、精准投放扶贫金融产品、精准开展直联直扶等方式，切实推进金融精准扶贫。同时，以创新推动普惠金融，主动承担了地方政府“创业贷款”的落地实施；开通了“汇享付”一码多付扫码支付业务，在重点乡镇区域布设了集存取款、转账、充值缴费等功能于一体的“服农通”助农终端53台，打通了农村金融服务最后一公里。二是提升公司品牌形象。积极利用各类媒体，优质高效做好对外宣传，加大正面宣传报道力度，向莱芜银监分局、市政府信息科、市人行、金融办、莱芜银行业协会等单位报送信息150余条次，采用40余条，在《莱芜日报》每周五金融周刊中发布信息30余篇，正向引导社会舆论，提升了莱商银行社会形象。同时在莱芜电视台“赢在金融”栏目进行形象和业务产品宣传，取得较好效果。配合市金融办、人民银行、银监局组织开展消费者权益保护、金融知识进校园、金融知识普及等各类宣传活动，切实维护金融消费者权益。积极配合市委创城办做好创城工作，组织人员参加交通路口指引、开展“窗口单位”规范化建设等工作，并荣获“创建全国文明城市工作集体三等功”。三是积极参与各类慈善公益事业。与莱芜日报社等单位联合举办三次“徒步雪野、相约四季”徒步大赛活动，促进全民健身，倡导健康生活方式，受到广大市民的普遍好评。冠名“莱商行 学子情”2017全市希望工程圆梦行动，共捐助63名大学新生，捐助金额18.9万元。组织员工参加义务献血、春蕾计划、慈心一日捐等活动，积极践行社会责任，社会知名度和公信力得到提升。四是注重人本管理。修订完善人事管理制度，上线人力资源管理系统，强化人事标准化管理，提高精细化管理水平，逐步实现全行科学、规范、高效的人力资源管理。建立全行后备干部队伍，为全行人力资源梯队建设奠定基础。通过民主推荐和组织考察的方式，选拔总行、分行、支行级后备干部66人，并组织

参加后备干部培训，为全行人才队伍建设提供了有力保障，有效达到了夯实后备干部理论基础、提高政治水平、锤炼党性修养、强化德才素质的培训目的。开展内训师推荐、评选工作，选出了一批在不同层面具有较为丰富的管理、实践经验及在特定业务领域具有良好理论基础或专项技能的内训师，为加强2018年全行内部培训工作奠定了良好基础。组织全行业务培训，结合全行2017年工作目标和各业务条线培训需求，制定了莱商银行2017年度培训计划，按月调度培训计划执行情况，确保了各项培训工作有序开展。

## 二、报告期内主要财务会计数据

## （一）报告期内贷款投放情况

## 四、2017年董事会工作计划

## (一) 工作思路：

以党的十九大精神和习近平新时代中国特色社会主义思想为指导，继续按照“一个中心、两个坚持、三个提升、四个加强”的战略部署，高举高质量稳健发展大旗，继续坚持稳中求进的总基调，继续实施“改革提质、创新提效、管理提速、党建保障”四轮驱动，突出“提高发展质量”和“防范化解风险”两大主题，紧扣合规经营这一总要求，努力做到“四个全面”，即全面提升基础管理水平，全面加大对新旧动能转换的支持力度，全面推进资产质量攻坚工作，全面提高团队综合素质，全力以赴保持高质量稳健发展的良好势头。

（二）工作目标：

1. 确保资本充足率达到监管要求；

2. 存款总额达到740.7亿元，同比增加65.7亿元，增幅9.74%，存款偏离度符合监管要求；

3. 贷款总额达到516.1亿元，同比增加54亿元，增幅11.68%；

4. 实现拨备前利润（税前）14.11亿元；

5. 实现安全营运全年无道德案件和重大责任案件发生。

## （三）工作重点

1. 进一步加强公司治理建设。董事会将严格按照监管要求，进一步梳理完善公司治理相关制度，将党建嵌入到公司治理的方方面面，梳理“三重一大”事项的决策流程，对公司章程、股东大会和董事会议事规则以及各专业委员会工作细则进行一次系统的修订与补充，厘清党委会、股东大会、董事会、监事会及各专业委员会的职责边界和运作流程，进一步提高“四会一层”运行

的规范化程度，优化公司治理决策、监督和制衡机制。另外，董事会将加强专业委员会建设，制定每个专业委员会的年度工作计划并切实执行，组织开展到分支机构和重点客户调研活动，提高董事履职水平，积极履行各专业委员会的核心职能。

2. 聚焦战略重点，促进转型发展。董事会将针对全行经营发展的新特点和外部环境发生的新变化，制定完善全行三年战略发展规划。把有效落实战略、推进转型发展作为核心工作，推动公司聚焦重点业务，打造专业优势，拓展新的盈利增长点。加强对战略执行情况的评估，定期研究战略执行过程中面临的突出问题和难点问题，支持高管层对战略重点业务加大资源投入，重点关注影响战略实施的关键能力建设，推动公司2018年在战略实施上取得较大进展。

3. 提升基础管理和基层管理水平。2018年董事会将把提升基础管理和基层管理作为一项重要举措，督导高管层切实改进。一是继续推进对标达标精细化管理，实现全面基础管理的提升，并将此项工作纳入总行部室的绩效合同，签订目标责任状。二是要夯实条线基础管理。以条线专业能力提升为重点，涵盖信贷、投资、财务、存款、结算等各个方面；三是要强化基层管理。重点抓行为规范、检查监督、培训整改，重点解决贷后管理、行为规范问题。

4. 落实全面风险管理。2018年，董事会将严格落实董事在风控方面的职责，制定《莱商银行2018年全面风险管理指导意见》，明确2018年风险管理政策、风险限额、风险偏好，并督导高管层将已确定的风险偏好、理念和策略在全行进行有效传导。董事会将持续关注风险管理的执行情况，督导管理层严把风控第一防线，加强检查与问责力度，深化降旧控新工作，提高全行合规经营意识。

5. 继续扎实推进资产质量攻坚 “334” 战略。持续完善不良信贷客户沙盘，逐户制定清收处置方案，并明确清收时间表和具体责任人。充分把握莱芜市委、市政府支持不良资产处置的有利契机，持续推进不良化解政策落地，强化现金清收，加大司法打击力度，加大已核销和抵债资产处置力度，通过资产转让、风险代理等渠道夯实不良资产处置实效。同时，要结合 “责任认定、新老划断、重在当下” 的原则，合理界定风险化解责任和风险清收责任，充分激发全行各级员工清收不良贷款的积极性。

6. 进一步完善资本补充机制。尽管公司 2017 年完成增资扩股并成功募集资金27.5亿元，但未来资本补充压力依然较大。为拓展发展空间，缓解 MPA 管理趋严带来的经营压力，董事会将认真执行《莱商银行股份公司资本规划（2018-2020）》，在坚持内生资本积累的同时，积极探索外源性资本补充方式，结合战略实施强化考核引导，着力提升资本使用效率，实现资本的优化配置，确保公司资本充足水平持续达标。

7. 多渠道提升品牌形象。通过对新时期企业发展过程中形成的优秀文化进行总结凝练，形成高质量稳健发展的发展文化、塑造“五年再造一个莱商”的战略文化、构建“关爱员工人本管理”的家文化，加强党建引领，通过多渠道的宣传，结合做好工会、妇联、共青团等方面的工作，对内对外树立良好的企业形象，提升品牌影响力。

### Chapter VII Report of the Board of Directors

## I. Review of main operation and management in 2017

## (i) The asset scale exceeded RMB 100 billion, and the operating results were improved significantly.

As of December 31, 2017, the Company's total assets reached RMB 103.034 billion; the balance of various deposits was RMB66.857 billion; the balance of various loans was RMB 46.872 billion; the profit before provision (pre-tax) was RMB 1.270 billion; the amount for extraction and provision was RMB 710 million; the total profit was RMB 560 million; the taxes paid reached RMB 531 million; the net asset value per share was RMB 2.91, so that the development growth ranked high among all city commercial banks in Shandong, the profit level was increased significantly, all kinds of risks were effectively controlled, all branches developed in different ways, and Laishang Bank presented a new vibrant and thriving atmosphere.

## (ii) The strategic decision-making functions were exerted and the capacity for development was enhanced continuously.

Firstly, the Company took the strategic opportunities accurately and ensured the steady progress of business development. The Board of Directors based on the overall situation, grasped the new requirements of the state for financial reform and innovation and service to the real economy, the profound changes in the important period of strategic opportunities, the development trend of the financial landscape, supervision and regulation domestically and abroad, the characteristics of the new stage for operation and development under new normal, scientifically determined the feasible annual business plan and management outlines, adhered to the prudent management, constantly created a new situation for reform and development, and promoted the business development ability, with business transformation as the core, credit management as the focus, and on the basis of customer service. Secondly, the Company adhered to innovative services and improved the quality and efficiency of supporting the real economy. The Board of Directors stuck to the basis of the real economy, tried to build the way of transformation for coordinate and sustainable development, made efforts to speed up the innovation of financial products and service mode, continuously raised the level of financial services for the real economy, met the needs of the economic transformation and upgrading and supply-side structural reform, strengthened the support of key construction projects, increased the credit support for the old and new kinetic energy transformation domain, backed key enterprises to do strongly and greatly, and cultivated new economic development momentum. Thirdly, the Company improved the organizational structure and network layout. The Company smoothly completed the internal structure reform, so that the departments /offices of the Head Office were optimized and merged from 19 to 15, the heads of all the departments / offices were hired newly after competition, all non-management personnel attended two-way selection and person-post matching, further improving the operational efficiency. At the same time, the Company

steadily advanced the construction of branches. In 2017, Mengyin Sub-branch (Linyi), Rizhao Development Zone Sub-branch (Rizhao), Longao Sub-branch (Jinan), Guohua Sub-branch (Heze), Zhuangzhai Mini Sub-branch (Cao County) etc. were opened. The deposits, loans and pre-provision profits of the branches in different places accounted for about 75% of the amounts of the whole bank, providing stronger and stronger support for the development of the whole bank. Fourthly, the Company strengthened the management of external investment. One the one hand, our bank strengthened the review of proposals of the Boards of Directors and Shareholders' General Meetings of the four village banks and other invested institutions, and the accredited directors and authorized agents issued review opinions at the meetings, so as to standardize the management process. Our Bank increase support for village banks, on the other hand, by dispatching excellent management personnel to participate in operation management and make important decisions, so that management was strengthened from the important positions and decision-making links. At moment a total of 17 management personnel were sent to village banks who have played an important role in the operation and management.

## (iii) The risk management was strengthened comprehensively and the overall stability of the asset quality was maintained.

Firstly, the Company promoted the construction of the internal control and compliance system by solidly carrying out the activities of Institutional Construction Year, highlighting the scientificity, applicability and effectiveness of systems, and revising a total of 368 systems, so as to basically form the system of internal rules and regulations with scientific content, rigorous process, and effective operation. The Company carried out internal audit in a planned way, by completing special audits in terms of authenticity of credit asset quality, line business, business authority, remuneration management, liquidity management, financial budget execution, protection of consumers' rights and interests, effectively grasped the problems existing in the operation and management in the Bank, put forward rectification measures, and ensured legal and compliable operation by strengthening the supervision and implementation. The Company completed the evaluation on internal control of 2016 and prepared the evaluation reports on internal control in the whole bank with the evaluation content mainly covering four aspects, i.e., profile of commercial bank, management and development of internal control, evaluation situation of internal control, and conclusions of internal control effectiveness, based on the daily supervision and special supervision of internal control according to the Internal Control Guidelines for Commercial Banks and other regulatory requirements, covers the business bank, so as to more objectively evaluate the overall situation of our Bank's internal control and put forward effective suggestions and recommendations to the management. Secondly, the Company steadily worked hard to improve the quality of assets and strengthen the disposal of non-performing assets. Strongly supported by the Municipal Party Committee, Municipal Government and Bank Regulatory Sub-bureaus, our Bank have made solid progress in improving asset quality by defining person in charge of disposal and disposal scheme for each asset and each account and really promoting the resolving of credit risks via working together with public security, court, real estate and other related departments. Thirdly, the Company strengthened the management of related transactions. The Board of Directors carried out comprehensive credit extension for Laiwu Steel Group Co., LTD., Xinwen Mining Group Co., Ltd., and their affiliated enterprises, and also the senior management submitted reports on related transactions to the Board of Directors on a

quarterly basis, and released the credit extension of employees and their stakeholders in the internal office automation system, re-formulated the Management Measures for Related Transactions so as to redefine affiliated legal person and affiliated natural person, advance the construction of the refined and standardized management mechanism of related transactions, further clearly define the management route, and ensure the compliance of related transactions. Fourthly, the Company built and perfected the comprehensive risk management system and improved the policy and institutional system of risk management by formulating the 2017 Annual Comprehensive Risk Management Policy of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. around the strategic planning of the whole Bank, so as to clarify the risk management policies, strategies and risk control objectives for the seven business lines. The Company strengthened the application of the credit risk management system, fully utilized the risk identification and early warning functions of the credit risk management system, and comprehensively improved the credit risk control ability of the whole Bank through the analysis and judgment of and timely disposal of early warning information of risks. The Company carried out the construction of the internal control compliance and operational risk management systems and on this basis further integrated the resources of internal control compliance and operational risk management, perfected the internal control compliance and operational risk management methods, platforms and tools, and timely recognized, evaluated, monitored, reported and controlled the operating risks.

## (iv) The capital management was strengthened and the capital adequacy ratio was overall stable.

Firstly, the Company successfully carried out capital increase and share expansion of RMB 1 billion. Guided by the regulatory departments at all levels, under the strong support of the shareholders, Laishang Bank Co., Ltd successfully completed the capital increase and share expansion of RMB 1 billion in December 2017, raising the capital of RMB 2.75 billion, and providing a solid capital for supporting the transformation and upgrade of the real economy and promote the high-quality steady development of the whole Bank. Secondly, the Company successfully completed the transfer of the strategic investor. Supported and Promoted by the Municipal Party Committee, Municipal Government, Shandong Bank Regulatory Bureau and Laiwu Bank Regulatory Sub-bureaus, Qilu Transportation Development Group accepted the transfer of all shares held in Laishang Bank Co., Ltd from Shanghai Pudong Development Bank in January 2017, becoming our new strategic investor. At present, the two parties have carried out overall cooperation in business integration, financial innovation and other areas, and next, will focus on promoting strategic cooperation in specific projects to fully give play to the role of the strategic investor and create a collaboration model therebetween. Thirdly, the Company continued to strengthen the capital planning. In order to further improve the capital use efficiency, improve the capital supplement and constraint mechanism, promote the healthy, sustainable and rapid development of business, the Board of Directors set up the optimal capital goal starting from the minimum regulatory requirements of capital, in combination with the practical situation of the Company, and reserving the capital buffer space, estimated the relevant indicators, forecast the capital shortfall in the next three years, and defined the early warning value of the capital adequacy indicators, proposed the capital supplementary plan, and formed the "Three-year Capital Planning of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. (2018-2020)".

(v) The social responsibilities were actively fulfilled and the brand value was enhanced.

Firstly, the Company continued to strengthen the targeted poverty alleviation and inclusive finance. By following the targeted poverty reduction strategy of the Municipal Party committees and governments at all levels, the Company gave full play to the leverage of financial poverty alleviation, continuously improved the top design, and solidly promoted the targeted financial poverty alleviation by actively innovating the mode of financial poverty alleviation and through accurate docking financial demand, accurate support of leading enterprises for poverty alleviation, accurate release of financial products for poverty alleviation, and accurate implementation of direct linkage and direct help, etc. At the same time, with innovation to promote inclusive finance, the Company took the initiative to carry out the implementation of "start-up loan" of the local governments, launched the business of one code for multiple payment with "Huixiangfu Code", set up 53 "Funongtong" terminals of helping agriculture in the key rural towns which integrating deposit, withdrawal, transfer, charge, payment, and other functions, creating the rural financial services of the last mile. Secondly, the Company enhanced the brand image by making quality and efficient publicity externally and increasing the intensity of positive publicity with all kinds of media. The Company submitted more than 150 messages to Laiwu Bank Regulatory Sub-bureau, Information Department of Municipal Government, Municipal People's Bank, Finance Office, Laiwu Banking Association and other units, among which more than 40 were adopted, and also released more than 30 articles in the Financial Weekly of Laiwu Daily which was published every Friday, to guide public opinion and promote the social image of Laishang Bank. At the same time, the Company carried on the publicity of image and business products in "Win in Finance" Program of Laiwu TV, achieving better results. Cooperating with the Municipal Financial Office, People's Bank, and Bank Regulatory Bureau, the company organized and carried out various propaganda activities for protecting the rights and interests of consumers, entering of financial knowledge into campus, financial knowledge popularization and so on, to safeguard really financial consumers' rights and interests. The Company actively cooperated with the Municipal Party Committee Office of City Building to do a good job in the building, organized personnel to participate in the traffic intersection guidance, conducted the standardization construction of "Window Organization", and won the "Third-class Collective Merits of Creating National Civilized City". Thirdly, the Company actively participated in various charity and public undertakings, held the Hiking Contest of "Hiking in Xueye around the Four Seasons" for three times jointly with Laiwu Daily Press and other units to promote the nationwide fitness and advocate a healthy lifestyle, which won general high praise from the public. The Company also named the 2017 Dream-realizing Activity of Hope Project "Love to Student from Laishang Bank", making contribution of a total of RMB 189,000 to 63 freshmen who were enrolled by universities and colleges. The Company organized the employees to participate in voluntary blood donation, Spring Bud Program, Charity Donation of One-day Salary and other activities, actively fulfilling social responsibility and promoting the social awareness and credibility. Fourthly, the Company paid attention to humanistic management by revising and perfecting the personnel management system, going online the human resource management system, strengthening the standardized HR management, improving the level of fine management, and gradually achieving the scientific, standardized and highly efficient HR management. The Company set up a team of reserved cadres and laid the foundation for the construction of the human resource echelon of the

whole Bank by selecting 66 reserved cadres for the head office, branches and sub-branches via democratic recommendation and organizational investigation, and organizing them to take part in the training of reserved cadres, so as to provide a powerful guarantee for the talent team construction of the whole bank, effectively achieve the training purposes of solidifying the theoretical foundation, improving the political level, cultivating the party spirit, and strengthening the ability and quality of the reserved cadres. The Company carried out recommendation and selection of internal trainers, picking out a batch of internal trainers in different levels who have richer experience in management and practice and good theoretical basis or special skills in particular business areas, laying a good foundation to strengthen the internal training work in 2018. The Company organized the business training in the whole Bank, developed the 2017 Annual Training Plan of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. combining the work goals of the whole Bank and training needs of each business line, and scheduled and the implemented the training plan on a monthly basis, ensuring that the training work was conducted orderly.

## IV. 2017 Work Plan of the Board of Directors

#### (i) Thinking of work

Guided by the spirit of the 19th National Congress of the Communist Party of China and Xi

Jinping's Concept of Socialist Ideology with Chinese Characteristics in the New Era, the Company's Board of Directors continued to follow the strategic deployment of "One Center, Two Persistence, Three Promotion, Four Strengthening", highly hold the banner of High quality and steady development, insist on the overall tone of "making progress while ensuring stability", implement the policies of "reform-driven quality, innovation-based efficiency, management efficiency and Party building assurance", highlight the major themes of "Improving development quality and preventing and solving risks", closely fulfill the general requirement of compliant operation, make efforts to realize "Four comprehensively" i.e., comprehensively enhance the level of basic management, comprehensively support the conversion between old and new kinetic energy, comprehensively promote the asset quality, and comprehensively improve the overall quality of the team and maintain a good momentum of sustainable, sound and rapid development.

#### (ii) Work objectives

1. Ensuring that capital adequacy ratio should meet the regulatory requirements;

2. Ensuring the total deposits hit RMB 74.07 billion, representing an increase of RMB 6.57 billion or 9.74%, and deposit deviation should meet the regulatory requirements;

3. The total loans are planned to hit RMB 51.61 billion, representing an increase of RMB 5.4 billion or 11.68% by the end of year.

4. Realizing a profit of RMB 1.411 billion before provision (before taxes);

5. Realizing safe operation and absence of moral case and major responsibility case in the whole year.

### (iii) Work focus

1. To further strengthen corporate governance. The Board of Directors will further comb and improve the relevant systems of corporate governance in strict accordance with the regulatory requirements, embed the CPC building into all aspects of corporate governance, comb the decision-making process of "three major & one big" matters, revise and supplement the Company's Articles of Association, the rules of procedure of the shareholders' general meeting and Board of Directors, and working rules of the professional committees, clarify the responsibility boundaries and operation process of the CPC committee meeting, shareholders' general meeting, Board of Directors, Board of Supervisors, further improve the degree of standardization of "four meeting and one level", optimize the decision-making, supervision and balance mechanism of corporate governance. In addition, the Board of Directors will strengthen the construction of the professional committees, prepare and really carry out an annual work plan for each professional committee, organize and conduct investigation and research activities to the branches and key customers, improve the level of the directors' Duty Performance, and positively fulfill the core functions of each professional committee.

2. To focus on the strategic priorities and promote the transformation and development. The Board of Directors will set up a three-year strategic development plan for the whole bank based on

the new characteristics of the operation and development of the whole bank and the new changes in the external environment; promote the Company to focus on key businesses, create professional advantages and expand new profit growth points with effective implementation of the strategies and promotion of the transformation and development as the core work; strengthen the assessment of strategic implementation, regularly research the problems and difficulties faced the outstanding in the implementation process of the strategy, support the senior management in increasing the resource strategic investment in major strategic key businesses, focus on the construction of the key ability influencing the strategy implementation, and promote the Company to make great progress on the strategy implementation in 2018.

3. To improve the basic management and its level. In 2018, the Board of Directors will take the promotion of basic management and grassroots management as an important measure to supervise the effective improvement of the senior management. Firstly, the Board of Directors will continue to promote the fine management of meeting standards, achieve the overall improvement of basic management, and include this work in the performance contract of the departments and offices of the head office, and sign the papers of target responsibility. Secondly, the Board of Directors will consolidate the basic management of the business lines by focusing on the improvement of professional ability of the business lines covering credit, investment, finance, deposit, settlement and other aspects. Thirdly, the Board of Directors will strengthen grass-roots management by focusing on the code of conduct, inspection and supervision, training and rectification, and also mainly resolving problems with post-loan management and code of conduct.

4. To implement comprehensive risk management. In 2018, the Board of Directors will strictly implement the responsibility of the directors in terms of risk control, develop the 2018 Guidelines for Overall Risk Management of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. to clarify the risk management policy, risk limits, and risk preference in 2018, and supervise the senior management to deliver the risk preference, concept and strategy which have been identified effectively in the whole Bank; continue to focus on the implementation of risk management, supervise the management in strict implementation of the first line of risk control, strengthen inspection and accountability, deepen the work of dropping the old and controlling the new, and increase the awareness of compliant operation of the whole Bank.

5. To continue to make solid progress in promoting the "334" strategy of asset quality. The Board of Directors will continue to improve the customer sand table of non-performing loans, set up the settlement and disposal plan for each client, and clearly define the timetable and specific responsible person; fully grasp the favorable opportunity where the Laiwu Municipal Party Committee and Municipal Government support the disposal of bad assets, advance the policy implementation to resolve bad assets, strengthen the cash collection, intensify judicial crackdown, intensify the disposal of the assets which have been written-off and paid for debts, and solidify the effect of bad assets disposal through asset transfer, risk agency and other channels. At the same time, the Board of Directors will reasonably define the resolving responsibility and risk collection.

responsibility and fully stimulate the enthusiasm of the employees at all levels to collect non-performing loans in combination with the principles of confirming responsibility, differentiating the old and the new, focusing on the present".

6. To further improve the capital replenishment mechanism. Although the Company completed the capital increase and share expansion in 2017 and successfully raised RMB 2.75 billion, the capital replenishment pressure is still large in the future. To expand the development space and alleviate the operation pressure brought by the tighter MPA management, the Board of Directors will conscientiously implement the Capital Planning of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. (2018-2020), insist on accumulation endogenous capital and while actively exploring the way to replenish external capital, strengthen the assessment guidance combining with the strategy implementation, and try to improve the capital use efficiency so as to achieve the optimal allocation of capital and ensure that the Company's capital adequacy levels continued to meet the requirements.

7. To promote the brand image through will multiple channels. The Board of Directors will, through summarizing the excellent culture forming in the process of enterprise development in the new period, Form a culture of development with high quality and steady development, shape the strategic culture of "reshaping a new Laishang Bank in five years", construct the family culture of "caring for employees & people-first management", strengthen the leading role of CPC building, and also establish a good corporate image at home and abroad and enhance the brand influence through multi-channel publicity and in combination with doing a good job in the Trade Unions, Women's Federation, and Communist Youth League.

## 二、监事会就有关事项发表的独立意见

## （一）公司依法经营情况

报告期内，公司坚持依法、合规经营，规范管理，经营决策程序符合法律、法规及公司章程的有关规定，董事及高级管理人员在业务经营及管理过程中谨慎、认真、勤勉，未发现违法违规违章行为和损害公司、股东利益行为。

## （二）财务报告真实性

2017年度财务报告已由中兴华会计师事务所根据《企业会计制度》和《企业会计准则》有关规定进行审计，并出具标准无保留意见审计报告。财务报告真实、客观、公允地反映了公司的财务状况和经营成果。

## （三）关联交易情况

公司的关联交易主要是股东贷款。监事会认为，报告期内，公司在处理关联交易时，严格遵守有关规定，履行关联交易操作程序，交易过程体现了公平合理原则，关联交易符合商业原则，未发现损害股东权益和公司利益的行为。

## （四）内部控制制度情况

监事会听取和审阅了《公司2016年度内部控制评价报告》，认为公司制定了较为完整、合理、有效的内部控制制度。

## （五）股东大会决议执行情况

监事会对董事会提交股东大会审议的各项报告和提案进行了查阅和调研，没有异议。监事会对股东大会决议的执行情况进行了监督，认为董事会按照公司治理要求，认真执行了股东大会的有关决议。

### II. Independent opinion on relevant issues from the Board of Supervisors

#### (i) Legal operation of the Company

During the reporting period, the Company has stuck to operation in accordance with laws and regulations and normalization of the management. The decision-making procedure met the related provisions of laws, regulations and the Articles of Association. The directors and the senior management were able to conscientiously implement their duties. No director or senior management has been discovered to have breached any laws or regulations or Articles of Association or harmed the interest of the Company when they execute their functions.

##### (ii) Authenticity of financial reports

Zhongxinghua Certified Public Accountants LLP has carefully audited the 2017 Annual Financial Report in accordance with Accounting System for Enterprises and Accounting Standard for Enterprises and issued standard unqualified audit report. The financial report has truthfully and fairly reflected the Company's financial position and operation results.

##### (iii) The related transactions

The related transactions of the Company are mainly shareholders' loans. The Board of

Supervisors think that during the reporting period when dealing with related transactions, the Company has strictly observed related regulations and performed the operating procedure of related transactions. Transaction processes have reflected the principle of fairness and justifiableness. The related transactions meet the commercial principles and no action compromising the interests of the Company or shareholders has been discovered.

##### (iv) The internal control system

The Board of Supervisors heard and deliberated the 2016 Annual Internal Control Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., and believed that the Company has formulated the relatively complete, reasonable and effective internal control system.

## (v) Implementation of the resolutions approved in the shareholders' general meetings

The Board of Supervisors has reviewed and investigated all reports and proposals submitted by the Board of Directors to the Shareholders' General Meeting for deliberation, without any objection. The Board of Supervisors has supervised over the implementation of the resolutions approved in the shareholders' general meetings, and thinks that the Board of Directors of the Company is able to conscientiously implement the related resolutions of the shareholders' general meetings according to the requirements of corporate governance.

## 第九章 重要事项

## 一、公司持有其他公司股权和参股金融企业股权情况

公司2004年9月，向德州市商业银行（2010年6月更名为德州银行）投资3,000万元，投资比例为6%，并于2009年12月新增投资24,000万元，投资比例为12.32%；2005年向淄博市商业银行（2009年3月更名为齐商银行）投资2,000万元，投资比例为0.81%；2008年7月，向山东省城市商业银行合作联盟有限公司投资1000万元，占注册资本的7.14%，于2009年增加投资1000万元，占注册资本的7.41%，2011年12月增加投资2000万元，占注册资本的8.6%。2009年9月，向河南方城凤裕村镇银行有限责任公司投资510万元，占注册资本的51%，于2011年7月，追加投资510万元，占注册资本的51%，于2014年10月，追加投资510万元，占注册资本的51%，于2017年9月，追加投资1020万元，占注册资本的51%；2010年10月，向东营莱商村镇银行股份有限公司投资3300万元，占注册资本的51%，于2011年11月，追加投资5370万元，占注册资本的28.9%，于2015年4月，追加投资38.35万元，占注册资本的20%；2013年3月，向天津武清村镇银行股份有限公司投资9800万元，占注册资本的49%，于2014年12月，追加投资1900万元，占注册资本的39%，于2016年12月，追加投资5460万元，占注册资本的43.89%；2014年1月，向五台莱商村镇银行股份有限公司投资800万元，占注册资本的40%。

## 二、收购及出售资产、吸收合并事项

2017年度无重大收购、出售资产、吸收合并事项。

# Chapter IX Significant Events

## I. Equity of other companies held by the Company and equity participating in other financial enterprises

In September 2004, the Company invested RMB 30 million with Dezhou City Commercial Bank (renamed as Dezhou Bank in June 2010), at an investment proportion of 6%, and added a new investment of RMB 240 million in December 2009, raising the investment proportion to 12.32%; in 2005, the Company invested RMB 20 million with Zibo City Commercial Bank (renamed as Qishang Bank in March 2009), at an investment proportion of 0.81%; in July 2008, the Company invested RMB 10 million in Shandong Provincial City Commercial Bank Alliance Company, accounting for 7.14% of the registered capital, and added an investment of RMB 10 million in 2009, accounting for 7.41% of the registered capital and invested another RMB 20 million in December, 2011, totally accounting for 8.6% of the registered capital. In September 2009, the Company invested RMB 5.1 million in Henan Fangcheng Fengyu Village Bank Co., Ltd., accounting for 51% of the registered capital, added investment of RMB 5.1 million in July 2011, accounting for 51% of the registered capital and added investment of RMB 5.1 million in October 2014, accounting for 51% of the registered capital; in October 2010, the Company invested RMB 33 million in Dongying Laishang Village Bank Co., Ltd., accounting for 51% of the registered capital, added investment of RMB 53.70 million in November 2011, accounting for 28.9% of the registered capital and added investment of RMB 383,500 in April 2015, accounting for 20% of the registered capital; in March 2013, the Company invested RMB 98 million in Tianjin Wuqing Village Bank Co., Ltd., accounting for 49% of the registered capital, added investment of RMB 19 million in December 2014, accounting for 39% of the registered capital; added investment of RMB 54.6 million in December 2016, accounting for 43.89% of the registered capital; in January 2014, the Company invested RMB 8 million in Wutai Laishang Village Bank Co., Ltd., accounting for 40% of the registered capital.

### II. Acquisition and sell for assets and absorption and merger

There was no significant acquisition or sell for assets or absorption and merger in 2017.

At the end of the reporting period, the maximum credit extension balance for a single client was RMB 490,300,000, accounting for 4.89% of the total net capital of the Bank; that for a single associated legal person or other group client to which the organization belongs was RMB 889,789,400, accounting for 8.88% of the total net capital of the Bank; the extension balance for all affiliated parties was RMB 925,939,700, accounting for 9.24% of the total net capital of the Bank, all of which met the regulatory requirements.

The related transactions of the Company are fair and reasonable. The loan examination and approval procedure is carried out according to the current credit business examination and approval and management system of the Company. The loan interest rate is estimated according to the Company's interest rate pricing model, and implemented in line with the principle of being market-oriented, without damage to shareholders' rights and interests or the Company's interests. The operation and financial position of all related parties of the Company are good and able to perform all agreements with the Company. No non-performing asset of related transactions has been incurred during the reporting period.

### IV. Major fund raising of the Company

During the reporting period, the Company successfully completed capital increase and share expansion of 1 billion shares at the price of RMB 2.75 per share on December 29, 2017.

### V. The Company's employment and dismissal of CPAs

In 2017, the Company employed Zhongxinghua Certified Public Accountants LLP to audit 2016 financial statements of the Company.

### VI. Other important events

None.
